- **beweispflicht**: Evidence before every 'done': never claim work is finished, fixed, deployed or correct without fresh proof, and report the true status. Use before saying done, fixed, tests pass, «fertig», «läuft», «erledigt», «klappt», before delivering apps, games, APKs, files, numbers or research, before commits, deploys and releases, and when asked «funktioniert es?», «geht's jetzt?», «hast du's getestet?», «Beweis». Also use when the user asks to build, fix, update or deploy something («bau», «fix», «update», «neue Version»), so the result is verified before reporting back. Not for pure questions, explan… ([ui-edi/skills](https://github.com/ui-edi/skills/tree/HEAD/beweispflicht))
